Also independently found+fixed a separate pre-existing test-infra bug this file's flakiness exposed: vi.spyOn on an already-spied method (no restoreMocks/clearMocks configured project-wide) returns the SAME mock across tests in a file, so a later test's mock.calls[0] silently read an earlier test's call -- fixed with afterEach(vi.restoreAllMocks()), verified 10 consecutive runs deterministic before and after. tsc exit 0. CC-2 | ACK+FIXED | GO-0042 | NOW=cash-flow-leftover-unique-round3 | SHA=<healthz> | GO -- CLOSES: none -- GUARD lane. Shipped+merged GO-0041 (PR #17533, squash b2e093b29a) first. GO-0042-CASH-FLOW-MANUAL-PULL-RETRY-DUPLICATE-ENTRIES found+fixed: genuinely new mechanism on /cash-flow's Manual Daily Projections "Pull" action. Sequential createForecastEntry() POSTs (rate-limited 30/min) can partially succeed then throw on a real backlog pull (up to ~400 candidate rows); onSuccess invalidated the cache but onError didn't, so a natural retry rebuilt its in-memory dedupe set from STALE data and re-created already-committed rows -- if the retry then fully succeeds, the error clears and the UI silently ends up double-counting duplicated forecast entries with zero error shown. No unique DB constraint catches it either. Fixed: onError now invalidates the same query key; Pull button also gates on entriesQuery.isFetching to close the race where a retry beats the background refetch. New test 2/2 PASS (stateful mock proves the previously-committed row is never re-POSTed on retry), proven against unfixed baseline via stash. Hit the same recurring missing-ToastProvider gap (5th time this session). tsc exit 0. Never GL (non-GL planning table, explicitly firewalled from accounting per the route's own header comment). Never trigger_deploy. Continuing to GO-0043 same turn.

Fixed with a CASE WHEN gate on all 5 dollar metrics (only add the _sb side when it's a genuinely different load); re-ran the corrected query directly against live Neon prod AFTER the fix -- all 4 previously-doubled trips now match source exactly, all 9 others unchanged. CC-2 | FINDING | INVOICE-SENT-WITHOUT-AR-RECOGNITION-JE | PORT=9224 | SHA=1bfaaf2 | PR=#15665 (merged, board) | GO -- Chasing "why does the invoice detail page's GL/Journal-Entries section say empty" turned into a real, precisely-traced defect, filed to CC-1 (money/GL lane). Root cause via code trace of apps/backend/src/accounting/revrec-delivery-posting/poster.service.ts: revenue recognition runs Event 1 "earn" (gated on delivery evidence only) then Event 2 "bill" (DR A/R, gated on hasApprovedPodEvidence() -- a real dispatch.pod_documents row with status='approved'). The real defect: nothing stops an invoice from being created and SENT to a customer without the same POD evidence the GL requires to ever recognize that revenue, and the invoice detail page's generic "No journal entries linked yet (unposted or posting reversed)" gives the user zero indication why (missing POD vs. genuinely broken look identical). A CPA/auditor benchmark (QuickBooks/NetSuite parity, the owner's stated standard) would flag this immediately as a control-account-vs-subledger break. Two fix options given in the row: gate invoice-send on POD, or surface the real gate reason on the invoice detail page instead of the generic sentence; plus the separate source_transaction_type tagging fix on the Event-2 A/R leg.
